\",\n \"\",\n \"\n \n \n \n \n \",\n IF(\n ISNOTBLANK(SELECT(WalletTransactions[Amount], AND([Invoice_Code] = [_THISROW].[Invoice_Code], [Transaction_Type] = \"إيداع\"))),\n CONCATENATE(\"\"),\n \"\"\n ),\n IF(\n ISNOTBLANK(SELECT(WalletTransactions[Amount], AND([Invoice_Code] = [_THISROW].[Invoice_Code], [Transaction_Type] = \"سحب\"))),\n CONCATENATE(\"\"),\n \"\"\n ),\n \"
العملية	المبلغ	العمولة	الملاحظات
إيداع	\", SUM(SELECT(WalletTransactions[Amount], AND([Invoice_Code] = [_THISROW].[Invoice_Code], [Transaction_Type] = \"إيداع\"))), \"	\", SUM(SELECT(WalletTransactions[Wallet_Commission], AND([Invoice_Code] = [_THISROW].[Invoice_Code], [Transaction_Type] = \"إيداع\"))), \"	\", SELECT(WalletTransactions[Description], AND([Invoice_Code] = [_THISROW].[Invoice_Code], [Transaction_Type] = \"إيداع\")), \"
سحب	\", SUM(SELECT(WalletTransactions[Amount], AND([Invoice_Code] = [_THISROW].[Invoice_Code], [Transaction_Type] = \"سحب\"))), \"	\", SUM(SELECT(WalletTransactions[Wallet_Commission], AND([Invoice_Code] = [_THISROW].[Invoice_Code], [Transaction_Type] = \"سحب\"))), \"	\", SELECT(WalletTransactions[Description], AND([Invoice_Code] = [_THISROW].[Invoice_Code], [Transaction_Type] = \"سحب\")), \"
\"\n ),\n \"\"\n ),\n IF(\n ISNOTBLANK(SELECT(ServiceEntry[Invoice_Code], [Invoice_Code] = [_THISROW].[Invoice_Code])),\n CONCATENATE(\n \"

\",\n TEXT(SELECT(ServiceEntry[Servicename], [Invoice_Code] = [_THISROW].[Invoice_Code])),\n \"	\",\n SUM(SELECT(ServiceEntry[Amount], [Invoice_Code] = [_THISROW].[Invoice_Code])),\n \"	\",\n SUM(SELECT(ServiceEntry[Paper_Count], [Invoice_Code] = [_THISROW].[Invoice_Code])),\n \"

DU]"},{"ColumnToEdit":"Work days","NewColumnValue":"=(\n DAY(\n EOMONTH(\n DATE(\n INDEX(SPLIT([Month], \" \"), 2) & \"/01/\" & INDEX(SPLIT([Month], \" \"), 1)\n ),\n 0\n )\n )\n)\n-\n(\n 1 +\n FLOOR(\n (\n DAY(\n EOMONTH(\n DATE(\n INDEX(SPLIT([Month], \" \"), 2) & \"/01/\" & INDEX(SPLIT([Month], \" \"), 1)\n ),\n 0\n )\n )\n -\n DAY(\n DATE(\n INDEX(SPLIT([Month], \" \"), 2) & \"/01/\" & INDEX(SPLIT([Month], \" \"), 1)\n )\n +\n MOD(\n (\n SWITCH(\n UPPER([Day Off]),\n \"SUNDAY\", 1,\n \"MONDAY\", 2,\n \"TUESDAY\", 3,\n \"WEDNESDAY\", 4,\n \"THURSDAY\", 5,\n \"FRIDAY\", 6,\n \"SATURDAY\", 7,\n 0\n )\n -\n WEEKDAY(\n DATE(\n INDEX(SPLIT([Month], \" \"), 2) & \"/01/\" & INDEX(SPLIT([Month], \" \"), 1)\n )\n )\n + 7\n ),\n 7\n )\n )\n ) / 7\n )\n)"},{"ColumnToEdit":"Required Hours","NewColumnValue":"=[Deleted Employee].[Shift Hours DU] * [Work days]"},{"ColumnToEdit":"Hourly Rate","NewColumnValue":"=[Monthly Salary]/[Required Hours]"},{"ColumnToEdit":"Achieved Hours","NewColumnValue":"=SUM(\n SELECT(\n Shift[Total_Hours],\n AND(\n [Shift_Month] = [_THISROW].[Month],\n [Employee_Id] = [_THISROW].[Deleted Employee]\n )\n )\n)"},{"ColumnToEdit":"Bonus hours","NewColumnValue":"=SUM(\n SELECT(\n Employee HR[Hours],\n AND(\n [Month] = [_THISROW].[Month],\n [E_HR_Id] = [_THISROW].[Deleted Employee],\n [Approval] = \"موافقة\",\n [Hr Items] <> \"خصم\"\n )\n )\n)"},{"ColumnToEdit":"Deducted Hours","NewColumnValue":"=SUM(SELECT(Employee HR[Hours], AND([Month] = [_THISROW].[Month], [E_HR_Id] = [_THISROW].[Deleted Employee], [Approval] = \"موافقة\",[Hr Items]=\"خصم\")))"},{"ColumnToEdit":"Final Hours","NewColumnValue":"=([Achieved Hours]+[Bonus hours])-[Deducted Hours]"},{"ColumnToEdit":"Hours Value","NewColumnValue":"=[Hourly Rate]*[Final Hours]"},{"ColumnToEdit":"Employee Commission","NewColumnValue":"=sum(select(ServiceEntry[Employee_Commission],and([Month]=[_THISROW].[Month],[Employee_Id]=[_thisrow].[Deleted Employee])))"},{"ColumnToEdit":"Total Advances","NewColumnValue":"=sum(select(Expenses[Amount],and([Month]=[_THISROW].[Month],[E_finances_Id]=[_thisrow].[Deleted Employee],[Type]=\"سلفة\")))"},{"ColumnToEdit":"Total Salary","NewColumnValue":"=sum(select(Expenses[Amount],and([Month]=[_THISROW].[Month],[E_finances_Id]=[_thisrow].[Deleted Employee],[Type]=\"قبض\")))"},{"ColumnToEdit":"Account","NewColumnValue":"=([Hours Value]+[Employee Commission])-([Total Advances]+[Total Salary])"}],"ColumnToEdit":"Monthly Salary","NewColumnValue":"=[Deleted Employee].[Salary
